# BLUE ARCHIVE JAPANESE VOICE AND ADDRESS LEDGER

## Current boundary

All **480 / 480** canonical main units inventoried in the [2026-09-28 source reconciliation](../01%20Source%20Lock%20and%20Inventory/BLUE_ARCHIVE_SOURCE_RECONCILIATION_20260928.md) at `electricgoat/ba-data@jp a038020f1f5ac02dcfe76962426d38f86414cdd8` now have admitted readings. Earlier completed readings retain their declared V1 witness; this reconciliation does not substitute source text. The final backfill reading is `MAIN_V001_C003 E043` / `BA:main:001:003:043`, governed by the [V001 C003 checkpoint](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/MAIN/VOLUME_001_%E5%AF%BE%E7%AD%96%E5%A7%94%E5%93%A1%E4%BC%9A%E7%B7%A8/BLUE_ARCHIVE_MAIN_V001_C003_CHECKPOINT.md). The latest forward released unit in this pinned snapshot remains `MAIN_S2_V003_C001 E014` / `BA:main:series2:003:001:014`, governed by the [S2 V003 C001 checkpoint](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/MAIN/SERIES2_VOLUME_003/BLUE_ARCHIVE_MAIN_S2_V003_C001_CHECKPOINT.md). No unopened main unit remains in the pinned snapshot; extending it requires a release and provenance recheck.

Cycles001–005 admit exactly **180 supplemental objects with limits:65 group,43 event,30 bond,30 MomoTalk and12 character_data**. [Cycle005](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/BLUE_ARCHIVE_PHASE2_CONTEXTUALIZATION_CYCLE_005_CHECKPOINT.md) and the exact object crosswalk own admission. Current combined coverage is **23 PARTIAL_MODEL /482 UNMODELED /505 analytical subjects**, every standalone model NONE. All65 group objects have complete accepted readings; Serika/Reijo/Rei/Ayane private/written pools have scoped acceptance; other private and mini obligations remain unadmitted. Main chronology, performed voice and unresolved outcomes retain their limits.

All ensuing unit and checkpoint entries retain the knowledge available at their stated reading position, including their uses of “current,” “now,” “unopened,” provisional identities and unresolved questions. These historical states are not a consolidated 480-unit endpoint; consult the relevant later deltas and chapter checkpoints for subsequent developments. Historical source witnesses and denominators remain unchanged.

## E008 delta — `BA:main:001:001:008`

### Financial / institutional vocabulary

- `会計担当` — Serika's explicit committee role; finances are not merely a shared worry but assigned governance work.
- `破産の寸前` — Serika's description of the school's fiscal condition: on the verge of bankruptcy.
- `利息だけで788万円` — monthly interest alone is ¥7.88 million.
- `らちが明かない` — current incremental efforts will not resolve the problem; motivates jackpot thinking.
- `一攫千金` — getting rich in one stroke / striking it rich; vocabulary of desperate shortcut economics.
- `マルチ商法` — multi-level marketing; Ayane's categorical identification of Serika's bracelet scheme.
- `世間知らず` — Hoshino's description of Serika as inexperienced/naive about the world.
- `悪い大人` — explicit negative adult category; supports adult-status versus responsible-adult distinction.
- `生徒の数イコール学校の力` — Hoshino's compact institutional thesis linking enrollment to school power.
- `発言権` — voice/say/standing in the Federal Student Council, here linked by Hoshino to student population and representatives.

### Problem Solver 68 corporate / contract vocabulary

- `社長` / `室長` / `社員` — corporate-role performance inside the four-person organization; titles structure status even when material conditions are precarious.
- `襲撃任務` — Kayoko's unambiguous term for the current mission: an assault assignment.
- `ほぼ全財産` — almost all assets; operational commitment is materially costly to the contractor.
- `あらゆるリソースを総動員` — Aru's maximalist all-resources mobilization formula.
- `想定内` — Aru's claim that adverse circumstances are within expectations; recurrent mastery-performance marker in this bounded baseline.
- `人生の醍醐味` — Mutsuki's positive valuation of unpredictability as the real pleasure of life.

### Poverty, food, and dignity

- `お金がないのは罪じゃないよ！胸を張って！` — Serika explicitly separates poverty from moral guilt/shame.
- `お金は天下の回りもの` — money circulates; Serika uses a familiar proverb-like formulation to normalize temporary scarcity.
- `厚意に甘えて` — Aru recognizes the oversized serving as kindness and says the group should accept it gratefully.

### Voice baselines newly source-safe in E008

- **Aru:** executive/grandiose declaratives, role-title insistence, mastery rhetoric, and sudden sincere gratitude coexist.
- **Mutsuki:** familiar `アルちゃん`, teasing puncture of hierarchy, playful metacommentary, and explicit delight in unpredictability.
- **Kayoko:** economical, practical questioning around cost, risk, disturbance, and target proportionality.
- **Haruka:** highly deferential, apologetic, and extreme self-abasing escalation around poverty/status.

### E008 attribution caution

- `u:0089` / `DataList[1400]` and `u:0103` / `DataList[1413]` are internally inconsistent Nonomi mappings and remain quarantined.
- The Problem Solver 68 restaurant section from `u:0139` onward is internally coherent and usable for the four individual voice baselines above.
- `その制服、ゲヘナ？` is Shiroko's observation/question; do not convert it into a normalized school-affiliation fact at this boundary.


## E009 delta — `BA:main:001:001:009`

### Competing obligation vocabularies

- `いい人たちだった` — Aru positively evaluates Abydos before learning the restaurant students are the target; later hesitation is therefore moral/social personalization, not merely tactical surprise.
- `心優しいアルちゃん` — Mutsuki explicitly names Aru's kindness; supports a bounded trait claim without erasing Aru's subsequent agency in ordering the assault.
- `一企業の長として` — Aru invokes office/capacity (`～として`) to discipline her own hesitation; corporate role becomes active self-regulation.
- `情け無用` / `お金さえもらえればなんでもやります` — Mutsuki invokes claimed company hardness as a reason to proceed; slogan should not be treated as complete moral truth because Aru visibly struggles.
- `恩知らず` — Serika condemns the attackers for accepting hospitality and then attacking; extends her established reciprocity/`借り` ethic into `恩`-based relational obligation.
- `その件はありがと。それはそれ、これはこれ` — Mutsuki acknowledges the favor while explicitly separating it from the current job.
- `公私はハッキリ区別しないと` — Mutsuki frames private gratitude and professional duty as distinct domains; a clear current ethical proposition.
- `受けた仕事はきっちりこなす` — reliability/professionalism language applied to a paid assault, demonstrating that procedural professionalism is not identical to moral legitimacy.
- `企業秘密` — Aru treats the principal/client relation as protected company information, reinforcing intermediation/opacity.

### Labor / battle register

- `日雇いの傭兵` — Ayane identifies the approaching force as likely day-labor mercenaries.
- `残業はナシ` / `時給も値切られてる` — subcontractors define the work through overtime and negotiated hourly compensation.
- `定時` / `今日の日当だとここまで` — the battle stops when paid time ends.
- `退勤……いえ、退却` — Ayane's comic self-correction fuses employment and military vocabularies; both descriptions are structurally true.
- `逃げ……じゃなくて、退却` — Aru upgrades ordinary flight into dignified tactical terminology, continuing her linguistic self-fashioning.
- `三流悪役` — Mutsuki explicitly recognizes Aru's threat as genre performance rather than transparent menace.

### E009 attribution caution

- `scene:001:u:0003` / `DataList[1573]` is mapped to Nonomi but is semantically unstable (`あなたたちも学校の復興...`) in immediate context; exclude from fine-grained Nonomi voice inference.
- `scene:001:u:0052` / `DataList[1638]` is mapped to Aru while addressing `社長`, an implausible self-address; quarantine.
- `scene:001:u:0032` / `DataList[1613]` (`アル様！わっ、私、頑張りますから！`) is mapped to Mutsuki immediately before a Haruka continuation; treat cautiously and do not use as sole evidence for either voice.
- Anonymous mercenary lines are valid labor-register evidence but not stable individual voice profiles.

## E010 delta — `BA:main:001:001:010`

### Mutsuki / role-partition vocabulary

- `別に…嫌いなわけじゃない` — personal dislike is explicitly separated from operational hostility.
- `部活で請け負ってる仕事` — commissioned/undertaken club work; reinforces contractor status without specifying full terms.
- `仕事以外の時は仲良くしたっていい` — Mutsuki's explicit cross-context relation model.
- `今さら公私を区別` — Ayane throws the `公私` concept back at Mutsuki as a criticism of after-the-fact compartmentalization.
- `あんたたちだけのモンじゃない` — colloquial possessive framing used to deny Abydos exclusive ownership/access to Schale's Sensei.

### Debt / creditor vocabulary

- `変動金利` — variable interest becomes explicit creditor-side terminology.
- `788万3250円` — exact current interest collection.
- `全て現金` — cash-only payment is stated; purpose remains unknown.
- `カイザーローン` — named current creditor institution.
- `309年返済` — repayment horizon translated into human-timescale impossibility by Serika's `死ぬまで完済できない`.

### Investigation / institutional vocabulary

- `2つの事案` — Ayane explicitly preserves two cases rather than assuming one conspiracy.
- `現在は取引されていない型番` / `生産が中止` — forensic provenance vocabulary.
- `ブラックマーケット` — working procurement route for discontinued equipment in Ayane's current model.
- `中退・休学・退学` — multiple forms of disrupted school participation defining part of the Black Market population.
- `非認可の部活` — unapproved clubs operating without Federal Student Council permission.
- `関連性を探す` / `かもしれません` — explicit epistemic hedging: search for a connection rather than assert one.

### E010 attribution caution

- `u:0009` and `u:0012` map to Mutsuki's speaker ID but are internally unstable in the entrance sequence; neither is used for fine-grained Mutsuki voice inference.
- Convenience-layer `アル`/`ムツキ` labels in the committee briefing conflict with promoted person metadata and speaker mappings; the promoted layer controls current authority.
## E011 delta — `BA:main:001:001:011`

### Black Market / governance vocabulary

- `手が及ばない` — Shiroko describes an area beyond effective Federal Student Council reach; weaker/more precise than declaring the area lawless.
- `街ひとつぐらいの規模` — city-scale rather than a small illicit bazaar.

## C002 E001 delta — euphemized custody, jurisdiction, and reciprocal trust

- **Ako:** polished administrative mitigation—`協力をお願い`, `お迎え`, `庇護下`, `安全に確保`—euphemizes an operation backed by overwhelming force. Her register fractures into evasive informality when Hina calls and she claims to be on patrol near Gehenna.
- **Ayane:** `風紀委員会は委員会であって、生徒会ではありません` and `アビドスの自治権` make the jurisdictional distinction explicit; `指揮をお願いします` marks deliberate delegation to Sensei rather than surrender of policy.
- **Kayoko:** compressed inferential language converts troop scale and abnormal procedure into a diagnosis that Schale is the real objective.
- **Aru:** `信頼には信頼で報いる` supplies a formal reciprocity maxim that cuts through her outlaw performance, even as she continues to claim deliberate responsibility for the bombing.
- **Hina:** short interrogative pressure—why committee members are being moved independently outside the district—establishes supervisory authority and suspicion without narrating a full answer.
- **Key lexicon:** `自治権`, `庇護下`, `安全に確保`, `お迎え`, `信頼には信頼で報いる`, `指揮`.
- **Speaker anomalies:** scene 1 `u:0029-0032` alternates Ako/Iori across an internally inconsistent exchange, and scene 2 `u:0029`/`u:0034` are promoted as Hina although `委員長` address suggests otherwise. Preserve the exchange's structural content but quarantine exact speaker attribution and individual culpability.

## V100 C004 canonical checkpoint reconciliation

The [V100 C004 checkpoint](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/MAIN/VOLUME_100_最終編/BLUE_ARCHIVE_MAIN_V100_C004_CHECKPOINT.md) reconciles E001-E012 at **371 / 480** canonical main units. The coalition's east/west/lower-device intervention severs the ship/Ark link at one second; the OS reports temporary ground-Sanctum cancellation, while E008 OS voices report present Sensei safely returned after E007's dangerous fall. Plana is the existing control-room OS subject, distinct from familiar Arona; local and alternate Shiroko remain distinct. The exact duel, Ark/ship damage, Prenapates' fate, Sensei medical status, the alternate's durable status and wider casualty audit are unverified. E009-E012 supply civic repair reports, ambiguous `Kei.sav`, homecoming, pending Rin vote, unauthenticated Kuzunoha/president letters, Francis's unperformed Gematria plan and Plana's privately asserted president hypothesis. No new readiness promotion or standalone model: **21 PARTIAL_MODEL / 182 UNMODELED across 203**. No durable new claim ID, frozen analytical prediction, held-out diagnostic (`NO_DIAGNOSTIC_OPPORTUNITY`) or side-source admission. The 43 inserted V001 C003 units remain **DEFER** for their own ordered backfill. Next forward source: `BA:main:series2:000:001:001` / `MAIN_S2_V000_C001_E001`.

## S2 V000 C001 canonical checkpoint reconciliation

The [S2 V000 C001 checkpoint](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/MAIN/SERIES2_VOLUME_000/BLUE_ARCHIVE_MAIN_S2_V000_C001_CHECKPOINT.md) reconciles all four sequential readings at **375 / 480**. Decalcomania claims a new route to authority through an unheard telescreen without demonstrated power; Rin's unsigned “letter” proves to be a photo envelope with no message, including an unlocated monochrome lake; Sensei's monochrome city and unidentified girl are in a narrator-driven dream, followed by waking Arona/Plana care; Rei's dive team retrieves a shard she names a shining trapezohedron without composition, hazard or sponsor verification. No causal bridge between screen, photograph, dream and shard is printed. Four directly speaking dive-team subjects were added as narrow UNMODELED: **21 PARTIAL_MODEL / 186 UNMODELED across 207**. No durable new claim ID, standalone model, frozen analytical prediction, held-out diagnostic (`NO_DIAGNOSTIC_OPPORTUNITY`) or side-source admission. The 43 inserted V001 C003 episodes remain **DEFER** for ordered backfill. Next forward source: `BA:main:series2:001:001:001` / `MAIN_S2_V001_C001_E001`.
